# V837 Failure Ledger

Append-only research memory. Older failure entries and measurements must never be deleted or rewritten. Corrections are appended as new annotated entries. Every disproven hypothesis remains searchable.

## V837ak-BOUNDARY-INTERCHANGEABILITY-NOT-ESTABLISHED — V837ak / AK8

- **Hypothesis:** Held-out-confirmed and causal dynamical classes are boundary-interchangeable after identity/orthogonal alignment.
- **Result:** definitive within tested scope / SCIENTIFIC_FAILURE
- **Failed gate:** 0 boundary-interchangeable classes, 0 orthogonal rescues
- **Meaning:** Dynamical recurrence plus causal importance did not imply boundary interchangeability under V837ak's tested identity/orthogonal interfaces.
- **Do not repeat unchanged:** True
- **Source commit:** `7d3b70ee6907b5502e0f229116d74eff4745c71e`
- **Artifacts:** experiments/v837_primitive_invention/v837ak/diagnostics/causal_specificity.json, experiments/v837_primitive_invention/v837ak/raw/boundary_substitution_results.json

## V837al-STATIC-LINEAR-INTERFACE-EXHAUSTED — V837al / AL5

- **Hypothesis:** A compact context-independent linear transform of the six explicit boundary ports makes the powered causal motif reusable.
- **Result:** definitive within tested scope / SCIENTIFIC_FAILURE
- **Failed gate:** GLOBAL SELECT_PASS=0, CAUSAL SELECT_PASS=0
- **Meaning:** Static signed-permutation, scale/shift, rigid-affine and full-affine interface hypotheses are closed for the frozen V837al six-port formulation.
- **Do not repeat unchanged:** True
- **Source commit:** `7d3b70ee6907b5502e0f229116d74eff4745c71e`
- **Artifacts:** experiments/v837_primitive_invention/v837al/raw/scope_selection_results.json, experiments/v837_primitive_invention/v837al/diagnostics/decision_state.json

## V837am-A-STATIC-P1_OUTPUT — V837am / AM-A

- **Hypothesis:** Context-conditioned boundary translation makes the powered causal motif interoperable.
- **Result:** definitive within tested scope / SCIENTIFIC_FAILURE
- **Failed gate:** SELECT_EFFECT_GATE_FAIL, ADAPTER_LEARNS_COMPUTATION_NOT_INTERFACE
- **Meaning:** This exact conditioned interface configuration does not satisfy the frozen AM-A selection standard.
- **Do not repeat unchanged:** True
- **Source commit:** `7d3b70ee6907b5502e0f229116d74eff4745c71e`
- **Artifacts:** experiments/v837_primitive_invention/v837am/raw/am_a_selection.json

## V837am-ENG-GLOBAL-GATE-CONTEXT-POSITION-DRIFT — V837am / AM-A-IMPLEMENTATION

- **Hypothesis:** A single global scalar gate can be context-conditioned while preserving one position-invariant gate protocol.
- **Result:** implementation failure corrected before accepted science / ENGINEERING_FAILURE
- **Failed gate:** fit/application context semantics inconsistent for global gate
- **Meaning:** The implementation accidentally allowed position-dependent gate context during application despite fitting one global gate adapter.
- **Do not repeat unchanged:** True
- **Source commit:** `7d3b70ee6907b5502e0f229116d74eff4745c71e`
- **Artifacts:** experiments/v837_primitive_invention/v837am/context_replay.py, experiments/v837_primitive_invention/v837am/conditional_adapter.py, experiments/v837_primitive_invention/v837am/interface_data.py

## V837am-ENG-RANDOMIZED-PROJECTED-INPUT-TRACE — V837am / AM-A-IMPLEMENTATION

- **Hypothesis:** RANDOMIZED_REFIT must randomize every primitive-local floating parameter while preserving equal adapter capacity.
- **Result:** implementation failure corrected before accepted science / ENGINEERING_FAILURE
- **Failed gate:** randomized projected-input boundary did not reflect randomized projection parameters
- **Meaning:** The randomized-refit anti-cheating control was too favorable to the real donor at the projected-input port and therefore invalid for configurations using that port.
- **Do not repeat unchanged:** True
- **Source commit:** `7d3b70ee6907b5502e0f229116d74eff4745c71e`
- **Artifacts:** experiments/v837_primitive_invention/v837am/context_replay.py, experiments/v837_primitive_invention/v837am/conditional_adapter.py, experiments/v837_primitive_invention/v837am/interface_data.py

## V837am-ENG-LOWRANK-DENSE-DEPLOYMENT — V837am / AM-A-IMPLEMENTATION

- **Hypothesis:** CONTEXT_MOD_R1/R2/R4 must execute the compressed factorization whose parameter/MAC cost is reported.
- **Result:** implementation failure corrected before accepted science / ENGINEERING_FAILURE
- **Failed gate:** deployment representation did not match reported low-rank complexity
- **Meaning:** Any result from the old cache would mix dense scientific capacity with low-rank cost accounting and is inadmissible.
- **Do not repeat unchanged:** True
- **Source commit:** `7d3b70ee6907b5502e0f229116d74eff4745c71e`
- **Artifacts:** experiments/v837_primitive_invention/v837am/context_replay.py, experiments/v837_primitive_invention/v837am/conditional_adapter.py, experiments/v837_primitive_invention/v837am/interface_data.py

## REF-AN-001 — V837an / HISTORICAL_INTERPRETATION_REFINEMENT

- **Hypothesis:** V837am AM-A provided valid evidence for all 126 tested context-conditioned interface configurations.
- **Result:** interpretation refinement; historical diagnosis preserved / INTERPRETATION_REFINEMENT
- **Failed gate:** 105/126 configurations invalid before scientific effect gate
- **Meaning:** V837am remains valid within its declared interface gate, but most AM-A configurations cannot be cited as direct evidence against noninvertible causal macrovariables.
- **Do not repeat unchanged:** True
- **Source commit:** `f556c92a895b141f73f214e5eda3ac29b1927ea9`
- **Artifacts:** experiments/v837_primitive_invention/v837am/raw/am_a_selection.json, experiments/v837_primitive_invention/v837an/diagnostics/historical_interpretation_refinement.json

## REF-AN-002 — V837an / HISTORICAL_INTERPRETATION_REFINEMENT

- **Hypothesis:** V837am AM-C temporal/history candidates obtained valid intervention pairs and then failed the temporal effect gate.
- **Result:** interpretation refinement; historical diagnosis preserved / INTERPRETATION_REFINEMENT
- **Failed gate:** 0 valid evaluated pairs for all 7 AM-C configurations
- **Meaning:** The tested temporal-interface formulation could not satisfy its prerequisite state-map validity gate; temporal causal operators were not directly disproven.

- **Source commit:** `f556c92a895b141f73f214e5eda3ac29b1927ea9`
- **Artifacts:** experiments/v837_primitive_invention/v837am/raw/am_c_selection.json, experiments/v837_primitive_invention/v837an/diagnostics/historical_interpretation_refinement.json

## V837an-ENG-ANB-GLOBAL-FP-CLOSURE — V837an / AN-B_ENGINEERING_PREFLIGHT

- **Hypothesis:** Summing ten float32 split global-source GEMMs reconstructs the monolithic historical global term within the frozen 1e-6 gate.
- **Result:** engineering numerical-closure failure fixed before scientific routing measurements; no invalid science retained / ENGINEERING_FAILURE
- **Failed gate:** counterfactual global decomposition exceeded 1e-6 by float32 accumulation residual
- **Meaning:** Split-GEMM float32 accumulation order caused a preflight numerical closure miss; explicit deterministic residual closure restored exact frozen-gate equivalence without changing causal channels.
- **Do not repeat unchanged:** True
- **Source commit:** `f556c92a895b141f73f214e5eda3ac29b1927ea9`
